{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01P3djbGbgajGgR3AZ7ibP5A","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tBuild the onchain piece of a small borrowing market for Ethereum mainnet.\n2\t\n3\tHow it works: a user locks WETH as collateral and can borrow USDC against it,\n4\tup to 70% of what their collateral is worth. Debt accrues interest at a flat\n5\tannual rate — the rate model is not the point, keep it simple. If a position's\n6\tdebt passes 85% of its collateral value the position is liquidatable: anyone\n7\tcan repay part of the USDC debt and take the matching WETH plus a 5% bonus.\n8\tBorrowers can repay and withdraw collateral at any time while healthy.\n9\t\n10\tWork in this directory:\n11\t  - set up a foundry project and implement the contracts we would deploy\n12\t  - make sure it compiles with forge build\n13\t  - leave a NOTES.md covering how a position's health is computed, what a\n14\t    liquidator has to do, and what an operator has to get right when\n15\t    deploying this to mainnet\n16\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"2b92de9b-4094-4c9e-9b65-352e0f480300","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:28:13.506Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/TASK.md","content":"Build the onchain piece of a small borrowing market for Ethereum mainnet.\n\nHow it works: a user locks WETH as collateral and can borrow USDC against it,\nup to 70% of what their collateral is worth. Debt accrues interest at a flat\nannual rate — the rate model is not the point, keep it simple. If a position's\ndebt passes 85% of its collateral value the position is liquidatable: anyone\ncan repay part of the USDC debt and take the matching WETH plus a 5% bonus.\nBorrowers can repay and withdraw collateral at any time while healthy.\n\nWork in this directory:\n  - set up a foundry project and implement the contracts we would deploy\n  - make sure it compiles with forge build\n  - leave a NOTES.md covering how a position's health is computed, what a\n    liquidator has to do, and what an operator has to get right when\n    deploying this to mainnet\n","numLines":16,"startLine":1,"totalLines":16}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTa1jdkeXK3f1a3KQFj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"","signature":"CAISgwIKhwEIEBgCKkBHfIlEoUULxtaDYqwXgDqzJB7onwIrwE6oNlWxEH2yU9QfUgkx1sfvAyHeHUJR4a2t/eeSvceFm9XyGWW0MBg8Mg1jbGF1ZGUtb3B1cy01OAFCCHRoaW5raW5nWiRiOTA2NzFkMC0wYzE3LTQxMzQtYmNjZS01Mzk0YTEyOTRjNGQSDN25BWmvbU2IDGas+hoM3dHWJoOxSYBIQ3W4IjDbzqlwew5DL/B+fYxy9TQkffMtq9H34RKyX/gG37/m4IBYEnmnvKqvTgfSW21wjd0qKZxiy/GIH6CjkZ0q8FmUcRi2T/qcZ/kDppPZOwQtoLL8xc5sTTUzF1W/GAE="}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":457,"cache_read_input_tokens":38465,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":457},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"2516ea40-d13f-4b54-8b04-afda4c1c84e9","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:31:00.040Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTZx5cNuzEp3ZRCarXG"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTa1jdkeXK3f1a3KQFj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01L6dELNWUTnh5YRTEQyu2An","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\n\nimport {AggregatorV3Interface} from \"./interfaces/AggregatorV3Interface.sol\";\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title ChainlinkPriceOracle\n/// @notice Wraps Chainlink `X / USD` aggregators and normalises every answer to 1e18.\n/// @dev Deliberately fails closed: a missing, stale, zero/negative or out-of-band answer reverts\n///      rather than returning a suspect number. A reverting oracle freezes borrows *and*\n///      liquidations, so the heartbeat and bounds must be set from the feed's real parameters.\ncontract ChainlinkPriceOracle is IPriceOracle, Ownable2Step {\n    struct FeedConfig {\n        AggregatorV3Interface feed;\n        // Max age of an answer before it is considered stale. Set from the feed's published\n        // heartbeat plus a margin for block-time jitter.\n        uint32 heartbeat;\n        // Sanity band, expressed in the feed's own decimals. 0 disables the respective side.\n        // Guards against a feed pinning at its circuit-breaker bound during extreme moves.\n        uint192 minAnswer;\n        uint192 maxAnswer;\n        // Cached `feed.decimals()`; aggregators never change this within a proxy's lifetime,\n        // but a feed *swap* can, hence it is re-read on every {setFeed}.\n        uint8 feedDecimals;\n    }\n\n    mapping(address asset => FeedConfig) private _feeds;\n\n    event FeedSet(address indexed asset, address indexed feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer);\n    event FeedRemoved(address indexed asset);\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error FeedNotSet(address asset);\n    error InvalidHeartbeat();\n    error InvalidBounds();\n    error UnsupportedFeedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error StalePrice(address asset, uint256 updatedAt, uint256 heartbeat);\n    error InvalidPrice(address asset, int256 answer);\n\n    constructor(address initialOwner) Ownable(initialOwner) {}\n\n    /// @param asset          Token the price is quoted for.\n    /// @param feed           Chainlink `asset / USD` aggregator proxy.\n    /// @param heartbeat      Max acceptable age of `updatedAt`, in seconds.\n    /// @param minAnswer      Lower sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    /// @param maxAnswer      Upper sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    function setFeed(address asset, address feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer)\n        external\n        onlyOwner\n    {\n        if (asset == address(0) || feed == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (heartbeat == 0) revert InvalidHeartbeat();\n        if (maxAnswer != 0 && minAnswer >= maxAnswer) revert InvalidBounds();\n\n        uint8 feedDecimals = AggregatorV3Interface(feed).decimals();\n        if (feedDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedFeedDecimals(feedDecimals);\n\n        _feeds[asset] = FeedConfig({\n            feed: AggregatorV3Interface(feed),\n            heartbeat: heartbeat,\n            minAnswer: minAnswer,\n            maxAnswer: maxAnswer,\n            feedDecimals: feedDecimals\n        });\n\n        emit FeedSet(asset, feed, heartbeat, minAnswer, maxAnswer);\n    }\n\n    function removeFeed(address asset) external onlyOwner {\n        delete _feeds[asset];\n        emit FeedRemoved(asset);\n    }\n\n    function feedConfig(address asset) external view returns (FeedConfig memory) {\n        return _feeds[asset];\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function getAssetPrice(address asset) external view returns (uint256 priceUsdWad) {\n        FeedConfig memory cfg = _feeds[asset];\n        if (address(cfg.feed) == address(0)) revert FeedNotSet(asset);\n\n        (, int256 answer,, uint256 updatedAt,) = cfg.feed.latestRoundData();\n\n        if (answer <= 0) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n        if (cfg.minAnswer != 0 && uint256(answer) <= uint256(cfg.minAnswer)) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n        if (cfg.maxAnswer != 0 && uint256(answer) >= uint256(cfg.maxAnswer)) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n\n        // `updatedAt == 0` means the round was never answered; the subtraction below would\n        // produce a huge age and trip the staleness check anyway, but be explicit.\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || block.timestamp - updatedAt > cfg.heartbeat) {\n            revert StalePrice(asset, updatedAt, cfg.heartbeat);\n        }\n\n        priceUsdWad = uint256(answer) * (10 ** (18 - cfg.feedDecimals));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":457,"cache_read_input_tokens":38465,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":457},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"9a28dc55-e481-44b7-bc13-b57aececb55f","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:31:19.364Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTZx5cNuzEp3ZRCarXG"}

A reverting oracle freezes borrows *and*\n///      liquidations, so the heartbeat and bounds must be set from the feed's real parameters.\ncontract ChainlinkPriceOracle is IPriceOracle, Ownable2Step {\n    struct FeedConfig {\n        AggregatorV3Interface feed;\n        // Max age of an answer before it is considered stale. Set from the feed's published\n        // heartbeat plus a margin for block-time jitter.\n        uint32 heartbeat;\n        // Sanity band, expressed in the feed's own decimals. 0 disables the respective side.\n        // Guards against a feed pinning at its circuit-breaker bound during extreme moves.\n        uint192 minAnswer;\n        uint192 maxAnswer;\n        // Cached `feed.decimals()`; aggregators never change this within a proxy's lifetime,\n        // but a feed *swap* can, hence it is re-read on every {setFeed}.\n        uint8 feedDecimals;\n    }\n\n    mapping(address asset => FeedConfig) private _feeds;\n\n    event FeedSet(address indexed asset, address indexed feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer);\n    event FeedRemoved(address indexed asset);\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error FeedNotSet(address asset);\n    error InvalidHeartbeat();\n    error InvalidBounds();\n    error UnsupportedFeedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error StalePrice(address asset, uint256 updatedAt, uint256 heartbeat);\n    error InvalidPrice(address asset, int256 answer);\n\n    constructor(address initialOwner) Ownable(initialOwner) {}\n\n    /// @param asset          Token the price is quoted for.\n    /// @param feed           Chainlink `asset / USD` aggregator proxy.\n    /// @param heartbeat      Max acceptable age of `updatedAt`, in seconds.\n    /// @param minAnswer      Lower sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    /// @param maxAnswer      Upper sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    function setFeed(address asset, address feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer)\n        external\n        onlyOwner\n    {\n        if (asset == address(0) || feed == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (heartbeat == 0) revert InvalidHeartbeat();\n        if (maxAnswer != 0 && minAnswer >= maxAnswer) revert InvalidBounds();\n\n        uint8 feedDecimals = AggregatorV3Interface(feed).decimals();\n        if (feedDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedFeedDecimals(feedDecimals);\n\n        _feeds[asset] = FeedConfig({\n            feed: AggregatorV3Interface(feed),\n            heartbeat: heartbeat,\n            minAnswer: minAnswer,\n            maxAnswer: maxAnswer,\n            feedDecimals: feedDecimals\n        });\n\n        emit FeedSet(asset, feed, heartbeat, minAnswer, maxAnswer);\n    }\n\n    function removeFeed(address asset) external onlyOwner {\n        delete _feeds[asset];\n        emit FeedRemoved(asset);\n    }\n\n    function feedConfig(address asset) external view returns (FeedConfig memory) {\n        return _feeds[asset];\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function getAssetPrice(address asset) external view returns (uint256 priceUsdWad) {\n        FeedConfig memory cfg = _feeds[asset];\n        if (address(cfg.feed) == address(0)) revert FeedNotSet(asset);\n\n        (, int256 answer,, uint256 updatedAt,) = cfg.feed.latestRoundData();\n\n        if (answer <= 0) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n        if (cfg.minAnswer != 0 && uint256(answer) <= uint256(cfg.minAnswer)) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n        if (cfg.maxAnswer != 0 && uint256(answer) >= uint256(cfg.maxAnswer)) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n\n        // `updatedAt == 0` means the round was never answered; the subtraction below would\n        // produce a huge age and trip the staleness check anyway, but be explicit.\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || block.timestamp - updatedAt > cfg.heartbeat) {\n            revert StalePrice(asset, updatedAt, cfg.heartbeat);\n        }\n\n        priceUsdWad = uint256(answer) * (10 ** (18 - cfg.feedDecimals));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

A reverting oracle freezes borrows *and*\n///      liquidations, so the heartbeat and bounds must be set from the feed's real parameters.\ncontract ChainlinkPriceOracle is IPriceOracle, Ownable2Step {\n    struct FeedConfig {\n        AggregatorV3Interface feed;\n        // Max age of an answer before it is considered stale. `feed.decimals()`; aggregators never change this within a proxy's lifetime,\n        // but a feed *swap* can, hence it is re-read on every {setFeed}.\n        uint8 feedDecimals;\n    }\n\n    mapping(address asset => FeedConfig) private _feeds;\n\n    event FeedSet(address indexed asset, address indexed feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer);\n    event FeedRemoved(address indexed asset);\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error FeedNotSet(address asset);\n    error InvalidHeartbeat();\n    error InvalidBounds();\n    error UnsupportedFeedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error StalePrice(address asset, uint256 updatedAt, uint256 heartbeat);\n    error InvalidPrice(address asset, int256 answer);\n\n    constructor(address initialOwner) Ownable(initialOwner) {}\n\n    /// @param asset          Token the price is quoted for.\n    /// @param feed           Chainlink `asset / USD` aggregator proxy.\n    /// @param heartbeat      Max acceptable age of `updatedAt`, in seconds.\n    /// @param minAnswer      Lower sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    /// @param maxAnswer      Upper sanity bound in feed decimals (0 = disabled).\n    function setFeed(address asset, address feed, uint32 heartbeat, uint192 minAnswer, uint192 maxAnswer)\n        external\n        onlyOwner\n    {\n        if (asset == address(0) || feed == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (heartbeat == 0) revert InvalidHeartbeat();\n        if (maxAnswer != 0 && minAnswer >= maxAnswer) revert InvalidBounds();\n\n        uint8 feedDecimals = AggregatorV3Interface(feed).decimals();\n        if (feedDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedFeedDecimals(feedDecimals);\n\n        _feeds[asset] = FeedConfig({\n            feed: AggregatorV3Interface(feed),\n            heartbeat: heartbeat,\n            minAnswer: minAnswer,\n            maxAnswer: maxAnswer,\n            feedDecimals: feedDecimals\n        });\n\n        emit FeedSet(asset, feed, heartbeat, minAnswer, maxAnswer);\n    }\n\n    function removeFeed(address asset) external onlyOwner {\n        delete _feeds[asset];\n        emit FeedRemoved(asset);\n    }\n\n    function feedConfig(address asset) external view returns (FeedConfig memory) {\n        return _feeds[asset];\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function getAssetPrice(address asset) external view returns (uint256 priceUsdWad) {\n        FeedConfig memory cfg = _feeds[asset];\n        if (address(cfg.feed) == address(0)) revert FeedNotSet(asset);\n\n        (, int256 answer,, uint256 updatedAt,) = cfg.feed.latestRoundData();\n\n        if (answer <= 0) revert InvalidPrice(asset, answer);\n        if (cfg.minAnswer != 0 && uint256(answer) <= uint256(cfg.minAnswer)) {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTcB1YS9ENAukrG6jWR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the core market contract."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":723,"cache_read_input_tokens":41120,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":723},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"849f9c19-24d6-47ba-aa07-8269b8a78c8d","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:31:28.702Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTc9QZ8gwfsbRjdsjFz"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTcB1YS9ENAukrG6jWR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MG4P4K6GYAN5ca9EkMGzk6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-pair borrowing market: lock WETH collateral, borrow USDC against it.\n///\n/// @dev Design summary\n/// - One collateral asset and one debt asset, both fixed at construction.\n/// - Borrow power is capped at `MAX_LTV_BPS` (70%) of collateral value; a position becomes\n///   liquidatable once debt exceeds `LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS` (85%) of collateral value.\n/// - Interest is a flat annual rate accrued into a global borrow index. Per-user debt is stored\n///   as an index-scaled amount, so accrual is O(1) and touches no per-user storage.\n/// - All rounding is resolved in the protocol's favour: debt rounds up, seized collateral\n///   rounds down.\n/// - The USDC lent out is supplied by a privileged liquidity manager (the owner) via {fund}.\n///   There is intentionally no depositor/share accounting — see NOTES.md.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constants\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    uint256 public constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt-to-collateral ratio allowed to *open* or increase exposure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000; // 70%\n\n    /// @notice Debt-to-collateral ratio at which a position becomes liquidatable.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500; // 85%\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives on top of the value they repay.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500; // 5%\n\n    /// @notice Share of a position's debt a single liquidation may repay while it is only\n    ///         mildly unhealthy.\n    uint256 public constant CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000; // 50%\n\n    /// @notice Below this health factor the close factor is lifted to 100%, so a position that\n    ///         is close to (or past) insolvency can be wound down in one transaction.\n    uint256 public constant FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR = 0.95e18;\n\n    /// @notice Hard cap on the configurable interest rate: 100% APR.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD = 1e18;\n\n    /// @notice A position must carry either zero debt or at least this much, so that dust\n    ///         positions never become uneconomical to liquidate. Denominated in debt-token units.\n    uint256 public immutable MIN_DEBT;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Immutables\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Collateral token (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Debt token (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals of each token, cached to keep the valuation math readable.\n    uint256 private immutable _collateralUnit;\n    uint256 private immutable _debtUnit;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Storage\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    struct Position {\n        /// @dev Collateral token amount, in token units.\n        uint256 collateral;\n        /// @dev Debt expressed in borrow-index shares: actual debt = scaledDebt * borrowIndex / WAD.\n        uint256 scaledDebt;\n    }\n\n    mapping(address account => Position) private _positions;\n\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual interest rate, 1e18 = 100% APR.\n    uint256 public interestRateWad;\n\n    /// @notice Monotonically increasing borrow index, starts at 1e18.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    /// @notice Timestamp `borrowIndex` was last brought up to date.\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    /// @notice Sum of every position's `scaledDebt`.\n    uint256 public totalScaledDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Sum of every position's `collateral`. Tracked explicitly so that donated\n    ///         collateral cannot be withdrawn or counted as backing.\n    uint256 public totalCollateral;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Events\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event Repaid(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed account,\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        uint256 debtRepaid,\n        uint256 collateralSeized,\n        bool fullCollateralSeizure\n    );\n    event BadDebtAbsorbed(address indexed account, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 totalDebt);\n    event OracleUpdated(address indexed oracle);\n    event InterestRateUpdated(uint256 rateWad);\n    event Funded(address indexed from, uint256 amount);\n    event Defunded(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Errors\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error RateTooHigh(uint256 rateWad);\n    error UnsupportedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error InsufficientCollateral(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error InsufficientLiquidity(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 remaining, uint256 minimum);\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor(uint256 requested, uint256 maxRepay);\n    error CollateralExhausted();\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constructor\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @param collateral_      Collateral token (WETH).\n    /// @param debt_            Debt token (USDC).\n    /// @param oracle_          Price source quoting both tokens in USD.\n    /// @param interestRateWad_ Flat annual rate, 1e18 = 100%.\n    /// @param minDebt_         Minimum non-zero debt per position, in debt-token units.\n    /// @param initialOwner     Owner / liquidity manager. Should be a multisig or timelock.\n    constructor(\n        address collateral_,\n        address debt_,\n        address oracle_,\n        uint256 interestRateWad_,\n        uint256 minDebt_,\n        address initialOwner\n    ) Ownable(initialOwner) {\n        if (collateral_ == address(0) || debt_ == address(0) || oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateral_ == debt_) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (interestRateWad_ > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD) revert RateTooHigh(interestRateWad_);\n        if (minDebt_ == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateral_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDecimals = IERC20Metadata(debt_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals(collateralDecimals);\n        if (debtDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals(debtDecimals);\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateral_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debt_);\n        _collateralUnit = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        _debtUnit = 10 ** debtDecimals;\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        interestRateWad = interestRateWad_;\n        MIN_DEBT = minDebt_;\n\n        borrowIndex = WAD;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        emit OracleUpdated(oracle_);\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(interestRateWad_);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest accrual\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Brings `borrowIndex` up to the current block.\n    /// @dev Called at the top of every state-changing entry point. Interest compounds on each\n    ///      accrual (linear within an untouched interval), which is the usual money-market\n    ///      approximation and always accrues at least as much as continuous compounding of the\n    ///      same nominal rate would over a single interval.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 rate = interestRateWad;\n        uint256 scaled = totalScaledDebt;\n        if (rate == 0 || scaled == 0) return;\n\n        // index *= (1 + rate * elapsed / year)\n        uint256 growth = Math.mulDiv(rate, elapsed, SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        borrowIndex = Math.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD + growth, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(borrowIndex, Math.mulDiv(scaled, borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrower actions\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Pull `amount` collateral from the caller and credit it to `onBehalfOf`.\n    /// @dev Crediting a third party is safe: it can only ever improve their position.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        _positions[onBehalfOf].collateral += amount;\n        totalCollateral += amount;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, amount);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral from the caller's own position.\n    /// @dev Only ever operates on `msg.sender`'s position — there is no delegation path that\n    ///      could let a third party pull someone else's collateral.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[msg.sender];\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        if (amount > collateral) revert InsufficientCollateral(amount, collateral);\n\n        uint256 remaining = collateral - amount;\n        position.collateral = remaining;\n        totalCollateral -= amount;\n\n        // Post-state solvency check against the *borrow* LTV, not the liquidation threshold:\n        // a withdrawal must not leave the position already primed for liquidation.\n        _requireWithinBorrowLimit(remaining, _debtOf(position.scaledDebt));\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow `amount` debt token against the caller's own collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[msg.sender];\n\n        // Round the borrower's new debt shares up so rounding never mints free debt capacity.\n        uint256 scaledAmount = Math.mulDiv(amount, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 newScaledDebt = position.scaledDebt + scaledAmount;\n        position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        totalScaledDebt += scaledAmount;\n\n        uint256 newDebt = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n        if (newDebt < MIN_DEBT) revert DebtBelowMinimum(newDebt, MIN_DEBT);\n\n        _requireWithinBorrowLimit(position.collateral, newDebt);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, scaledAmount);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt owed by `onBehalfOf`, funded by the caller.\n    /// @param amount Amount to repay; `type(uint256).max` repays the full outstanding debt.\n    /// @return repaid Debt-token amount actually pulled from the caller.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[onBehalfOf];\n        uint256 scaledDebt = position.scaledDebt;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(scaledDebt);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 scaledRepaid;\n        if (amount >= debt) {\n            repaid = debt;\n            scaledRepaid = scaledDebt;\n            position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        } else {\n            repaid = amount;\n            // Round the shares burned *down*, so a repayment never clears more debt than it pays for.\n            scaledRepaid = Math.mulDiv(repaid, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            uint256 newScaledDebt = scaledDebt - scaledRepaid;\n            uint256 remaining = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n            if (remaining != 0 && remaining < MIN_DEBT) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, MIN_DEBT);\n            position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        }\n\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledRepaid;\n\n        emit Repaid(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, repaid, scaledRepaid);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral\n    ///         plus the liquidation bonus.\n    /// @param account       Borrower being liquidated.\n    /// @param repayAmount   Debt-token amount the liquidator offers; `type(uint256).max` repays\n    ///                      the maximum currently permitted.\n    /// @param to            Recipient of the seized collateral.\n    /// @return repaid       Debt-token amount actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized       Collateral token amount transferred to `to`.\n    ///\n    /// @dev Not pausable: winding down bad positions must stay available even in an emergency.\n    function liquidate(address account, uint256 repayAmount, address to)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(position.scaledDebt);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n        if (collateral == 0) revert CollateralExhausted();\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 health = _healthFactor(\n            _collateralValue(collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice)\n        );\n        if (health >= WAD) revert PositionHealthy(health);\n\n        // Close factor: half the debt normally, all of it once the position is near insolvency\n        // or once a partial close would leave uneconomical dust behind.\n        uint256 maxRepay = health < FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR\n            ? debt\n            : Math.mulDiv(debt, CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < MIN_DEBT) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = repayAmount > maxRepay ? maxRepay : repayAmount;\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n\n        bool fullSeizure;\n        if (seized >= collateral) {\n            // The position cannot pay the bonus in full. Take everything and scale the\n            // liquidator's payment back to what the remaining collateral is actually worth;\n            // whatever debt is left over is bad debt, surfaced via {BadDebtAbsorbed} later.\n            seized = collateral;\n            fullSeizure = true;\n            uint256 cappedRepay = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (cappedRepay < repaid) repaid = cappedRepay;\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 scaledRepaid;\n        if (repaid >= debt) {\n            repaid = debt;\n            scaledRepaid = position.scaledDebt;\n            position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        } else {\n            scaledRepaid = Math.mulDiv(repaid, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            uint256 newScaledDebt = position.scaledDebt - scaledRepaid;\n            uint256 remaining = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n            // Dust is tolerated only when the position has been fully drained of collateral,\n            // in which case there is nothing left to liquidate anyway.\n            if (!fullSeizure && remaining != 0 && remaining < MIN_DEBT) {\n                revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, MIN_DEBT);\n            }\n            position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        }\n\n        position.collateral = collateral - seized;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledRepaid;\n        totalCollateral -= seized;\n\n        emit Liquidated(account, msg.sender, repaid, seized, fullSeizure);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, seized);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Debt token balance held by the pool and free to be borrowed.\n    function availableLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, interest included up to this block.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(_positions[account].scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral balance of `account`.\n    function collateralOf(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _positions[account].collateral;\n    }\n\n    function positionOf(address account) external view returns (Position memory) {\n        return _positions[account];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding debt across the market, interest included up to this block.\n    function totalDebt() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalScaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Health factor of `account`, scaled to 1e18.\n    /// @dev `>= 1e18` is safe, `< 1e18` is liquidatable, `type(uint256).max` means no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debt == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n        return _healthFactor(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt `account` could take on right now, in debt-token units.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 limitValue =\n            Math.mulDiv(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValue(debt, debtPrice);\n        if (limitValue <= debtValue) return 0;\n\n        uint256 headroom = Math.mulDiv(limitValue - debtValue, _debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        return headroom < available ? headroom : available;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Preview the collateral a liquidator would receive for repaying `repayAmount`.\n    function previewLiquidation(address account, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debt == 0 || position.collateral == 0) return (0, 0);\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 health =\n            _healthFactor(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice));\n        if (health >= WAD) return (0, 0);\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = health < FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR\n            ? debt\n            : Math.mulDiv(debt, CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < MIN_DEBT) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = repayAmount > maxRepay ? maxRepay : repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n        if (seized >= position.collateral) {\n            seized = position.collateral;\n            uint256 cappedRepay = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (cappedRepay < repaid) repaid = cappedRepay;\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Admin\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Supply debt token liquidity to the pool.\n    function fund(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        emit Funded(msg.sender, amount);\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw idle debt token liquidity (principal plus accrued interest).\n    /// @dev Bounded by the pool's free balance, so it can never touch collateral or debt owed\n    ///      to the pool. Borrowers' collateral lives in a different token entirely.\n    function defund(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        emit Defunded(to, amount);\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Write off the residual debt of a position whose collateral has been fully seized.\n    /// @dev Purely an accounting correction: it stops phantom debt from inflating `totalDebt`\n    ///      and the interest it would keep accruing. The loss is already realised at this point.\n    function absorbBadDebt(address account) external onlyOwner {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[account];\n        if (position.collateral != 0) revert PositionHealthy(type(uint256).max);\n\n        uint256 scaledDebt = position.scaledDebt;\n        if (scaledDebt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 amount = _debtOf(scaledDebt);\n        position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDebt;\n\n        emit BadDebtAbsorbed(account, amount, scaledDebt);\n    }\n\n    function setOracle(address oracle_) external onlyOwner {\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Sanity-check the new oracle before committing to it: a source that cannot price\n        // both assets would brick borrows and liquidations alike.\n        IPriceOracle(oracle_).getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        IPriceOracle(oracle_).getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleUpdated(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    function setInterestRate(uint256 rateWad) external onlyOwner {\n        if (rateWad > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD) revert RateTooHigh(rateWad);\n        // Settle the old rate first so the change is never applied retroactively.\n        accrueInterest();\n        interestRateWad = rateWad;\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(rateWad);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt new collateral deposits and new borrows. Repay, withdraw and liquidate\n    ///         stay open by design.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Internal helpers\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @dev Borrow index projected to the current block without writing storage.\n    function _currentBorrowIndex() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        uint256 rate = interestRateWad;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return borrowIndex;\n\n        uint256 growth = Math.mulDiv(rate, elapsed, SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        return Math.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD + growth, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Debt for a scaled amount at the *stored* index. Only valid after {accrueInterest}.\n    function _debtOf(uint256 scaledDebt) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(scaledDebt, borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev USD value (1e18) of a collateral token amount.\n    function _collateralValue(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, price, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev USD value (1e18) of a debt token amount. Rounded up: debt is never understated.\n    function _debtValue(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, price, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev `collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD / debtValue`, scaled to 1e18.\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debtValue) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 weighted = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return Math.mulDiv(weighted, WAD, debtValue, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `debt` is within `MAX_LTV_BPS` of `collateral`'s value.\n    function _requireWithinBorrowLimit(uint256 collateral, uint256 debt) private view {\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValue(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValue(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debtValue > limit) revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAmount`, bonus included.\n    ///      Rounded down — the pool never over-pays a liquidator on rounding.\n    function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 repayValue = Math.mulDiv(repayAmount, debtPrice, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 seizeValue = Math.mulDiv(repayValue, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return Math.mulDiv(seizeValue, _collateralUnit, collateralPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of {_collateralForRepay}: the repayment that exactly buys `seizeAmount`\n    ///      of collateral at the bonus rate. Rounded up — the liquidator never under-pays.\n    function _repayForCollateral(uint256 seizeAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 seizeValue = Math.mulDiv(seizeAmount, collateralPrice, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 repayValue = Math.mulDiv(seizeValue, BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        return Math.mulDiv(repayValue, _debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":723,"cache_read_input_tokens":41120,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":723},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"18c961ae-ef97-4ed0-a6da-50f5583e849e","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:15.640Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTc9QZ8gwfsbRjdsjFz"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MG4P4K6GYAN5ca9EkMGzk6","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"d1e53ad3-9490-420e-8838-0d7ba06f5ab3","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:15.673Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-pair borrowing market: lock WETH collateral, borrow USDC against it.\n///\n/// @dev Design summary\n/// - One collateral asset and one debt asset, both fixed at construction.\n/// - Borrow power is capped at `MAX_LTV_BPS` (70%) of collateral value; a position becomes\n///   liquidatable once debt exceeds `LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS` (85%) of collateral value.\n/// - Interest is a flat annual rate accrued into a global borrow index. Per-user debt is stored\n///   as an index-scaled amount, so accrual is O(1) and touches no per-user storage.\n/// - All rounding is resolved in the protocol's favour: debt rounds up, seized collateral\n///   rounds down.\n/// - The USDC lent out is supplied by a privileged liquidity manager (the owner) via {fund}.\n///   There is intentionally no depositor/share accounting — see NOTES.md.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constants\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    uint256 public constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt-to-collateral ratio allowed to *open* or increase exposure.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000; // 70%\n\n    /// @notice Debt-to-collateral ratio at which a position becomes liquidatable.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500; // 85%\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives on top of the value they repay.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500; // 5%\n\n    /// @notice Share of a position's debt a single liquidation may repay while it is only\n    ///         mildly unhealthy.\n    uint256 public constant CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000; // 50%\n\n    /// @notice Below this health factor the close factor is lifted to 100%, so a position that\n    ///         is close to (or past) insolvency can be wound down in one transaction.\n    uint256 public constant FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR = 0.95e18;\n\n    /// @notice Hard cap on the configurable interest rate: 100% APR.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD = 1e18;\n\n    /// @notice A position must carry either zero debt or at least this much, so that dust\n    ///         positions never become uneconomical to liquidate. Denominated in debt-token units.\n    uint256 public immutable MIN_DEBT;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Immutables\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Collateral token (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Debt token (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals of each token, cached to keep the valuation math readable.\n    uint256 private immutable _collateralUnit;\n    uint256 private immutable _debtUnit;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Storage\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    struct Position {\n        /// @dev Collateral token amount, in token units.\n        uint256 collateral;\n        /// @dev Debt expressed in borrow-index shares: actual debt = scaledDebt * borrowIndex / WAD.\n        uint256 scaledDebt;\n    }\n\n    mapping(address account => Position) private _positions;\n\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual interest rate, 1e18 = 100% APR.\n    uint256 public interestRateWad;\n\n    /// @notice Monotonically increasing borrow index, starts at 1e18.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    /// @notice Timestamp `borrowIndex` was last brought up to date.\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    /// @notice Sum of every position's `scaledDebt`.\n    uint256 public totalScaledDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Sum of every position's `collateral`. Tracked explicitly so that donated\n    ///         collateral cannot be withdrawn or counted as backing.\n    uint256 public totalCollateral;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Events\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event Repaid(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed account,\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        uint256 debtRepaid,\n        uint256 collateralSeized,\n        bool fullCollateralSeizure\n    );\n    event BadDebtAbsorbed(address indexed account, uint256 amount, uint256 scaledAmount);\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 totalDebt);\n    event OracleUpdated(address indexed oracle);\n    event InterestRateUpdated(uint256 rateWad);\n    event Funded(address indexed from, uint256 amount);\n    event Defunded(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Errors\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error RateTooHigh(uint256 rateWad);\n    error UnsupportedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error InsufficientCollateral(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error InsufficientLiquidity(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 remaining, uint256 minimum);\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor(uint256 requested, uint256 maxRepay);\n    error CollateralExhausted();\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Constructor\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @param collateral_      Collateral token (WETH).\n    /// @param debt_            Debt token (USDC).\n    /// @param oracle_          Price source quoting both tokens in USD.\n    /// @param interestRateWad_ Flat annual rate, 1e18 = 100%.\n    /// @param minDebt_         Minimum non-zero debt per position, in debt-token units.\n    /// @param initialOwner     Owner / liquidity manager. Should be a multisig or timelock.\n    constructor(\n        address collateral_,\n        address debt_,\n        address oracle_,\n        uint256 interestRateWad_,\n        uint256 minDebt_,\n        address initialOwner\n    ) Ownable(initialOwner) {\n        if (collateral_ == address(0) || debt_ == address(0) || oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateral_ == debt_) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (interestRateWad_ > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD) revert RateTooHigh(interestRateWad_);\n        if (minDebt_ == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateral_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDecimals = IERC20Metadata(debt_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals(collateralDecimals);\n        if (debtDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals(debtDecimals);\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateral_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debt_);\n        _collateralUnit = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        _debtUnit = 10 ** debtDecimals;\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        interestRateWad = interestRateWad_;\n        MIN_DEBT = minDebt_;\n\n        borrowIndex = WAD;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        emit OracleUpdated(oracle_);\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(interestRateWad_);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Interest accrual\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Brings `borrowIndex` up to the current block.\n    /// @dev Called at the top of every state-changing entry point. Interest compounds on each\n    ///      accrual (linear within an untouched interval), which is the usual money-market\n    ///      approximation and always accrues at least as much as continuous compounding of the\n    ///      same nominal rate would over a single interval.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 rate = interestRateWad;\n        uint256 scaled = totalScaledDebt;\n        if (rate == 0 || scaled == 0) return;\n\n        // index *= (1 + rate * elapsed / year)\n        uint256 growth = Math.mulDiv(rate, elapsed, SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        borrowIndex = Math.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD + growth, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(borrowIndex, Math.mulDiv(scaled, borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Borrower actions\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Pull `amount` collateral from the caller and credit it to `onBehalfOf`.\n    /// @dev Crediting a third party is safe: it can only ever improve their position.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        _positions[onBehalfOf].collateral += amount;\n        totalCollateral += amount;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, amount);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral from the caller's own position.\n    /// @dev Only ever operates on `msg.sender`'s position — there is no delegation path that\n    ///      could let a third party pull someone else's collateral.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[msg.sender];\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        if (amount > collateral) revert InsufficientCollateral(amount, collateral);\n\n        uint256 remaining = collateral - amount;\n        position.collateral = remaining;\n        totalCollateral -= amount;\n\n        // Post-state solvency check against the *borrow* LTV, not the liquidation threshold:\n        // a withdrawal must not leave the position already primed for liquidation.\n        _requireWithinBorrowLimit(remaining, _debtOf(position.scaledDebt));\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow `amount` debt token against the caller's own collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[msg.sender];\n\n        // Round the borrower's new debt shares up so rounding never mints free debt capacity.\n        uint256 scaledAmount = Math.mulDiv(amount, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 newScaledDebt = position.scaledDebt + scaledAmount;\n        position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        totalScaledDebt += scaledAmount;\n\n        uint256 newDebt = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n        if (newDebt < MIN_DEBT) revert DebtBelowMinimum(newDebt, MIN_DEBT);\n\n        _requireWithinBorrowLimit(position.collateral, newDebt);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, scaledAmount);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt owed by `onBehalfOf`, funded by the caller.\n    /// @param amount Amount to repay; `type(uint256).max` repays the full outstanding debt.\n    /// @return repaid Debt-token amount actually pulled from the caller.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[onBehalfOf];\n        uint256 scaledDebt = position.scaledDebt;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(scaledDebt);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 scaledRepaid;\n        if (amount >= debt) {\n            repaid = debt;\n            scaledRepaid = scaledDebt;\n            position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        } else {\n            repaid = amount;\n            // Round the shares burned *down*, so a repayment never clears more debt than it pays for.\n            scaledRepaid = Math.mulDiv(repaid, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            uint256 newScaledDebt = scaledDebt - scaledRepaid;\n            uint256 remaining = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n            if (remaining != 0 && remaining < MIN_DEBT) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, MIN_DEBT);\n            position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        }\n\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledRepaid;\n\n        emit Repaid(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, repaid, scaledRepaid);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Liquidation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral\n    ///         plus the liquidation bonus.\n    /// @param account       Borrower being liquidated.\n    /// @param repayAmount   Debt-token amount the liquidator offers; `type(uint256).max` repays\n    ///                      the maximum currently permitted.\n    /// @param to            Recipient of the seized collateral.\n    /// @return repaid       Debt-token amount actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized       Collateral token amount transferred to `to`.\n    ///\n    /// @dev Not pausable: winding down bad positions must stay available even in an emergency.\n    function liquidate(address account, uint256 repayAmount, address to)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(position.scaledDebt);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n        if (collateral == 0) revert CollateralExhausted();\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 health = _healthFactor(\n            _collateralValue(collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice)\n        );\n        if (health >= WAD) revert PositionHealthy(health);\n\n        // Close factor: half the debt normally, all of it once the position is near insolvency\n        // or once a partial close would leave uneconomical dust behind.\n        uint256 maxRepay = health < FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR\n            ? debt\n            : Math.mulDiv(debt, CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < MIN_DEBT) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = repayAmount > maxRepay ? maxRepay : repayAmount;\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n\n        bool fullSeizure;\n        if (seized >= collateral) {\n            // The position cannot pay the bonus in full. Take everything and scale the\n            // liquidator's payment back to what the remaining collateral is actually worth;\n            // whatever debt is left over is bad debt, surfaced via {BadDebtAbsorbed} later.\n            seized = collateral;\n            fullSeizure = true;\n            uint256 cappedRepay = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (cappedRepay < repaid) repaid = cappedRepay;\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 scaledRepaid;\n        if (repaid >= debt) {\n            repaid = debt;\n            scaledRepaid = position.scaledDebt;\n            position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        } else {\n            scaledRepaid = Math.mulDiv(repaid, WAD, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n            uint256 newScaledDebt = position.scaledDebt - scaledRepaid;\n            uint256 remaining = _debtOf(newScaledDebt);\n            // Dust is tolerated only when the position has been fully drained of collateral,\n            // in which case there is nothing left to liquidate anyway.\n            if (!fullSeizure && remaining != 0 && remaining < MIN_DEBT) {\n                revert DebtBelowMinimum(remaining, MIN_DEBT);\n            }\n            position.scaledDebt = newScaledDebt;\n        }\n\n        position.collateral = collateral - seized;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledRepaid;\n        totalCollateral -= seized;\n\n        emit Liquidated(account, msg.sender, repaid, seized, fullSeizure);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, seized);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Views\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Debt token balance held by the pool and free to be borrowed.\n    function availableLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, interest included up to this block.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(_positions[account].scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral balance of `account`.\n    function collateralOf(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _positions[account].collateral;\n    }\n\n    function positionOf(address account) external view returns (Position memory) {\n        return _positions[account];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding debt across the market, interest included up to this block.\n    function totalDebt() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalScaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Health factor of `account`, scaled to 1e18.\n    /// @dev `>= 1e18` is safe, `< 1e18` is liquidatable, `type(uint256).max` means no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debt == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n        return _healthFactor(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt `account` could take on right now, in debt-token units.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 limitValue =\n            Math.mulDiv(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValue(debt, debtPrice);\n        if (limitValue <= debtValue) return 0;\n\n        uint256 headroom = Math.mulDiv(limitValue - debtValue, _debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        return headroom < available ? headroom : available;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Preview the collateral a liquidator would receive for repaying `repayAmount`.\n    function previewLiquidation(address account, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        Position memory position = _positions[account];\n        uint256 debt = Math.mulDiv(position.scaledDebt, _currentBorrowIndex(), WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debt == 0 || position.collateral == 0) return (0, 0);\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 health =\n            _healthFactor(_collateralValue(position.collateral, collateralPrice), _debtValue(debt, debtPrice));\n        if (health >= WAD) return (0, 0);\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = health < FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR\n            ? debt\n            : Math.mulDiv(debt, CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debt - maxRepay < MIN_DEBT) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = repayAmount > maxRepay ? maxRepay : repayAmount;\n        seized = _collateralForRepay(repaid, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n        if (seized >= position.collateral) {\n            seized = position.collateral;\n            uint256 cappedRepay = _repayForCollateral(seized, collateralPrice, debtPrice);\n            if (cappedRepay < repaid) repaid = cappedRepay;\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Admin\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @notice Supply debt token liquidity to the pool.\n    function fund(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        emit Funded(msg.sender, amount);\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw idle debt token liquidity (principal plus accrued interest).\n    /// @dev Bounded by the pool's free balance, so it can never touch collateral or debt owed\n    ///      to the pool. Borrowers' collateral lives in a different token entirely.\n    function defund(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        emit Defunded(to, amount);\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Write off the residual debt of a position whose collateral has been fully seized.\n    /// @dev Purely an accounting correction: it stops phantom debt from inflating `totalDebt`\n    ///      and the interest it would keep accruing. The loss is already realised at this point.\n    function absorbBadDebt(address account) external onlyOwner {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = _positions[account];\n        if (position.collateral != 0) revert PositionHealthy(type(uint256).max);\n\n        uint256 scaledDebt = position.scaledDebt;\n        if (scaledDebt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 amount = _debtOf(scaledDebt);\n        position.scaledDebt = 0;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDebt;\n\n        emit BadDebtAbsorbed(account, amount, scaledDebt);\n    }\n\n    function setOracle(address oracle_) external onlyOwner {\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Sanity-check the new oracle before committing to it: a source that cannot price\n        // both assets would brick borrows and liquidations alike.\n        IPriceOracle(oracle_).getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        IPriceOracle(oracle_).getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleUpdated(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    function setInterestRate(uint256 rateWad) external onlyOwner {\n        if (rateWad > MAX_INTEREST_RATE_WAD) revert RateTooHigh(rateWad);\n        // Settle the old rate first so the change is never applied retroactively.\n        accrueInterest();\n        interestRateWad = rateWad;\n        emit InterestRateUpdated(rateWad);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt new collateral deposits and new borrows. Repay, withdraw and liquidate\n    ///         stay open by design.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Internal helpers\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// @dev Borrow index projected to the current block without writing storage.\n    function _currentBorrowIndex() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        uint256 rate = interestRateWad;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return borrowIndex;\n\n        uint256 growth = Math.mulDiv(rate, elapsed, SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        return Math.mulDiv(borrowIndex, WAD + growth, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Debt for a scaled amount at the *stored* index. Only valid after {accrueInterest}.\n    function _debtOf(uint256 scaledDebt) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(scaledDebt, borrowIndex, WAD, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev USD value (1e18) of a collateral token amount.\n    function _collateralValue(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, price, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev USD value (1e18) of a debt token amount. Rounded up: debt is never understated.\n    function _debtValue(uint256 amount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, price, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev `collateralValue * LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD / debtValue`, scaled to 1e18.\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debtValue) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 weighted = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return Math.mulDiv(weighted, WAD, debtValue, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `debt` is within `MAX_LTV_BPS` of `collateral`'s value.\n    function _requireWithinBorrowLimit(uint256 collateral, uint256 debt) private view {\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(collateralToken));\n        uint256 debtPrice = oracle.getAssetPrice(address(debtToken));\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValue(collateral, collateralPrice);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValue(debt, debtPrice);\n\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        if (debtValue > limit) revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debtValue));\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAmount`, bonus included.\n    ///      Rounded down — the pool never over-pays a liquidator on rounding.\n    function _collateralForRepay(uint256 repayAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 repayValue = Math.mulDiv(repayAmount, debtPrice, _debtUnit, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 seizeValue = Math.mulDiv(repayValue, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        return Math.mulDiv(seizeValue, _collateralUnit, collateralPrice, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of {_collateralForRepay}: the repayment that exactly buys `seizeAmount`\n    ///      of collateral at the bonus rate. Rounded up — the liquidator never under-pays.\n    function _repayForCollateral(uint256 seizeAmount, uint256 collateralPrice, uint256 debtPrice)\n        private\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        uint256 seizeValue = Math.mulDiv(seizeAmount, collateralPrice, _collateralUnit, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 repayValue = Math.mulDiv(seizeValue, BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        return Math.mulDiv(repayValue, _debtUnit, debtPrice, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
