test_onlyOwnerAdmin() public {\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setInterestRate(0);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setOracle(address(oracle));\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.defund(1e6, bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.absorbBadDebt(alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_rateAboveCapReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.RateTooHigh.selector, 1e18 + 1));\n        pool.setInterestRate(1e18 + 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_defundCannotExceedFreeLiquidity() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        uint256 free = pool.availableLiquidity();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.defund(free + 1, owner);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.defund(free, owner);\n        assertEq(pool.availableLiquidity(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_pauseStopsBorrowingButNotRepayOrLiquidate() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.depositCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        // Repay and liquidate remain reachable.\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_setOracleRejectsSourceThatCannotPriceBothAssets() public {\n        ChainlinkPriceOracle empty = new test_donatedCollateralIsNotCredited() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        weth.transfer(address(pool), 5 ether); // straight donation, no bookkeeping\n\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), 10 ether);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10 ether);\n\n        // Donation gives Alice no extra borrow power.\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_liquidationNeverSeizesMoreThanCollateral(uint256 ethPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        ethPrice = bound(ethPrice, 200, 1_650); // low enough that the position is liquidatable\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 20_000e6);\n\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        _setEthPrice(ethPrice);\n\n        uint256 collateralBefore = pool.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, bob);\n\n        assertLe(seized, collateralBefore);\n        assertLe(repaid, repayAmount);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), collateralBefore - seized);\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), collateralBefore - seized);\n SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingPoolTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 weth;\n    MockERC20 usdc;\n    MockAggregator ethFeed;\n    MockAggregator usdcFeed;\n    ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle;\n    LendingPool pool;\n\n    address owner = address(0xA11CE0);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    uint256 constant RATE = 0.05e18; // 5% APR\n    uint256 constant MIN_DEBT = 100e6;\n    uint32 constant HEARTBEAT = 1 hours;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n\n        ethFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        usdcFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 1e8);\n\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(this));\n        oracle.setFeed(address(weth), address(ethFeed), HEARTBEAT, 0, 0);\n        oracle.setFeed(address(usdc), address(usdcFeed), 24 hours, 0, 0);\n\n        pool = new LendingPool(address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), RATE, MIN_DEBT, owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        pool.fund(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100 ether);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        weth.mint(bob, 100 ether);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // helpers\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function _aliceBorrows(uint256 collateral, uint256 debt) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(collateral, alice);\n        pool.borrow(debt, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function _setEthPrice(uint256 usdPrice) internal {\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(int256(usdPrice * 1e8));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // borrowing / LTV\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_borrowUpToMaxLtv() public {\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 collateral -> 70% = $14,000 borrow power.\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.maxBorrowable(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowAboveMaxLtvReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowBelowMinDebtReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 99e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.borrow(99e6, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_healthFactorAtMaxLtv() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        // 20,000 * 0.85 / 14,000 = 1.2142857...\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.healthFactor(alice), 1.214285e18, 1e12);\n    }\n\n    function test_noDebtIsInfinitelyHealthy() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.healthFactor(alice), type(uint256).max);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // withdrawal\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_withdrawBlockedWhenItBreaksLtv() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawAfterFullRepay() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6); // cover interest\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100 ether);\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), 0);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // interest\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_interestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        pool.accrueInterest();\n\n        // 5% APR for exactly one year, compounded once.\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.totalDebt(), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_debtOfProjectsInterestWithoutAccrual() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_rateChangeIsNotRetroactive() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.setInterestRate(0); // settles 5% first, then stops the clock\n\n        uint256 debtAfterYearOne = pool.debtOf(alice);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(debtAfterYearOne, 10_500e6, 1);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), debtAfterYearOne);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // repayment\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_partialRepayLeavingDustReverts() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.repay(14_000e6 - 50e6, alice); // would leave 50 USDC < MIN_DEBT\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_thirdPartyCanRepay() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        uint256 repaid = pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // liquidation\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_healthyPositionCannotBeLiquidated() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, bob);\n    }\n\n    function test_partialLiquidationRespectsCloseFactor() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // $16,000 collateral vs $14,000 debt -> HF = 16000*0.85/14000 = 0.971 (< ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(this));\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setOracle(address(empty));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // accounting invariants\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_donatedCollateralIsNotCredited() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        weth.transfer(address(pool), 5 ether); // straight donation, no bookkeeping\n\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), 10 ether);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10 ether);\n\n        // Donation gives Alice no extra borrow power.\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_liquidationNeverSeizesMoreThanCollateral(uint256 ethPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        ethPrice = bound(ethPrice, 200, 1_650); // low enough that the position is liquidatable\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 20_000e6);\n\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        _setEthPrice(ethPrice);\n\n        uint256 collateralBefore = pool.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, bob);\n\n        assertLe(seized, collateralBefore);\n        assertLe(repaid, repayAmount);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), collateralBefore - seized);\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), collateralBefore - seized);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

       ethPrice = bound(ethPrice, 200, 1_650); // low enough that the position is liquidatable","newString":"        // HF crosses 1 at 14,000 / (10 * 0.85) = $1,647.06 per ETH; stay strictly below it.\n        ethPrice = bound(ethPrice, 200, 1_640);","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingPoolTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 weth;\n    MockERC20 usdc;\n    MockAggregator ethFeed;\n    MockAggregator usdcFeed;\n    ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle;\n    LendingPool pool;\n\n    address owner = address(0xA11CE0);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    uint256 constant RATE = 0.05e18; // 5% APR\n    uint256 constant MIN_DEBT = 100e6;\n    uint32 constant HEARTBEAT = 1 hours;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n\n        ethFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        usdcFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 1e8);\n\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(this));\n        oracle.setFeed(address(weth), address(ethFeed), HEARTBEAT, 0, 0);\n        oracle.setFeed(address(usdc), address(usdcFeed), 24 hours, 0, 0);\n\n        pool = new LendingPool(address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), RATE, MIN_DEBT, owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        pool.fund(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100 ether);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        weth.mint(bob, 100 ether);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // helpers\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function _aliceBorrows(uint256 collateral, uint256 debt) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(collateral, alice);\n        pool.borrow(debt, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function _setEthPrice(uint256 usdPrice) internal {\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(int256(usdPrice * 1e8));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // borrowing / LTV\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_borrowUpToMaxLtv() public {\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 collateral -> 70% = $14,000 borrow power.\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.maxBorrowable(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowAboveMaxLtvReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowBelowMinDebtReverts() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 99e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.borrow(99e6, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_healthFactorAtMaxLtv() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        // 20,000 * 0.85 / 14,000 = 1.2142857...\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.healthFactor(alice), 1.214285e18, 1e12);\n    }\n\n    function test_noDebtIsInfinitelyHealthy() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.healthFactor(alice), type(uint256).max);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // withdrawal\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_withdrawBlockedWhenItBreaksLtv() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawAfterFullRepay() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6); // cover interest\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100 ether);\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), 0);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // interest\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_interestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        pool.accrueInterest();\n\n        // 5% APR for exactly one year, compounded once.\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.totalDebt(), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_debtOfProjectsInterestWithoutAccrual() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_rateChangeIsNotRetroactive() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.setInterestRate(0); // settles 5% first, then stops the clock\n\n        uint256 debtAfterYearOne = pool.debtOf(alice);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(debtAfterYearOne, 10_500e6, 1);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), debtAfterYearOne);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // repayment\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_partialRepayLeavingDustReverts() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.repay(14_000e6 - 50e6, alice); // would leave 50 USDC < MIN_DEBT\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_thirdPartyCanRepay() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        uint256 repaid = pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // liquidation\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_healthyPositionCannotBeLiquidated() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, bob);\n    }\n\n    function test_partialLiquidationRespectsCloseFactor() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // $16,000 collateral vs $14,000 debt -> HF = 16000*0.85/14000 = 0.971 (< 1, but > 0.95)\n        _setEthPrice(1600);\n        assertLt(pool.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n        assertGt(pool.healthFactor(alice), pool.FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR());\n\n        uint256 bobWethBefore = weth.balanceOf(bob);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, bob);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6, \"close factor caps repayment at 50%\");\n        // $7,000 * 1.05 = $7,350 of WETH at $1600 = 4.59375 WETH\n        assertApproxEqAbs(seized, 4.59375 ether, 1e6);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob) - bobWethBefore, seized);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 7_000e6);\n        // Liquidation restores health.\n        assertGt(pool.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_fullLiquidationWhenDeeplyUnhealthy() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // $15,000 collateral vs $14,000 debt -> HF = 0.910 < 0.95\n        _setEthPrice(1500);\n        assertLt(pool.healthFactor(alice), pool.FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR());\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, bob);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6, \"whole debt closable below the full-liquidation threshold\");\n        // $14,000 * 1.05 = $14,700 at $1500 = 9.8 WETH\n        assertApproxEqAbs(seized, 9.8 ether, 1e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0.2 ether, 1e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationCapsSeizureAndLeavesBadDebt() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Collateral worth $10,000 against $14,000 of debt: insolvent.\n        _setEthPrice(1000);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, bob);\n\n        assertEq(seized, 10 ether, \"all collateral seized\");\n        // Liquidator pays only what the collateral is worth net of the bonus: 10,000 / 1.05\n        assertApproxEqAbs(repaid, 9_523_809_524, 10);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n\n        uint256 badDebt = pool.debtOf(alice);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(badDebt, 14_000e6 - 9_523_809_524, 10);\n\n        // A collateral-less position cannot be liquidated further.\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.CollateralExhausted.selector);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1e6, bob);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.absorbBadDebt(alice);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(pool.totalDebt(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidatorCannotSeizeMoreThanOffered() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        _setEthPrice(1600);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, bob);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 1_000e6);\n        // $1,000 * 1.05 / $1600 = 0.65625 WETH\n        assertApproxEqAbs(seized, 0.65625 ether, 1e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_previewMatchesLiquidation() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        _setEthPrice(1600);\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = pool.previewLiquidation(alice, type(uint256).max);\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, bob);\n\n        assertEq(previewRepaid, repaid);\n        assertEq(previewSeized, seized);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // oracle\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_stalePriceBlocksBorrow() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.depositCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + HEARTBEAT + 1);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_negativePriceReverts() public {\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(-1);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(ChainlinkPriceOracle.InvalidPrice.selector, address(weth), int256(-1)));\n        oracle.getAssetPrice(address(weth));\n    }\n\n    function test_priceOutsideSanityBandReverts() public {\n        oracle.setFeed(address(weth), address(ethFeed), HEARTBEAT, 100e8, 10_000e8);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(50_000e8);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        oracle.getAssetPrice(address(weth));\n    }\n\n    function test_priceNormalisedTo18Decimals() public view {\n        assertEq(oracle.getAssetPrice(address(weth)), 2000e18);\n        assertEq(oracle.getAssetPrice(address(usdc)), 1e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_usdcDepegShrinksDebtValue() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        uint256 healthAtPeg = pool.healthFactor(alice);\n\n        usdcFeed.setAnswer(0.9e8);\n        assertGt(pool.healthFactor(alice), healthAtPeg, \"cheaper debt token means a healthier position\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // admin / access control\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_onlyOwnerAdmin() public {\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setInterestRate(0);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setOracle(address(oracle));\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.defund(1e6, bob);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.absorbBadDebt(alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_rateAboveCapReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.RateTooHigh.selector, 1e18 + 1));\n        pool.setInterestRate(1e18 + 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_defundCannotExceedFreeLiquidity() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        uint256 free = pool.availableLiquidity();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.defund(free + 1, owner);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.defund(free, owner);\n        assertEq(pool.availableLiquidity(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_pauseStopsBorrowingButNotRepayOrLiquidate() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.depositCollateral(1 ether, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        // Repay and liquidate remain reachable.\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10 ether, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_setOracleRejectsSourceThatCannotPriceBothAssets() public {\n        ChainlinkPriceOracle empty = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(this));\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.setOracle(address(empty));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // accounting invariants\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    function test_donatedCollateralIsNotCredited() public {\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        weth.transfer(address(pool), 5 ether); // straight donation, no bookkeeping\n\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), 10 ether);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10 ether);\n\n        // Donation gives Alice no extra borrow power.\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_liquidationNeverSeizesMoreThanCollateral(uint256 ethPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        ethPrice = bound(ethPrice, 200, 1_650); // low enough that the position is liquidatable\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 20_000e6);\n\n        _aliceBorrows(10 ether, 14_000e6);\n        _setEthPrice(ethPrice);\n\n        uint256 collateralBefore = pool.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, bob);\n\n        assertLe(seized, collateralBefore);\n        assertLe(repaid, repayAmount);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), collateralBefore - seized);\n        assertEq(pool.totalCollateral(), collateralBefore - seized);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":424,"oldLines":7,"newStart":424,"newLines":8,"lines":["     }"," ","     function {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTvyhRt1j1dgxBRyFBC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011LmbsRu1rVSApRdsQ9LbWu","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122802Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/NOTES.md","content":"# WETH/USDC borrowing market — operator notes\n\nTwo contracts:\n\n- `src/LendingPool.sol` — the market. WETH collateral, USDC debt, flat-rate interest.\n- `src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol` — Chainlink `X/USD` feeds normalised to 1e18, fails closed.\n\n`script/Deploy.s.sol` wires both to mainnet addresses. `forge build` and `forge test` are green\n(30 tests).\n\n## Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Value | Where |\n|---|---|---|\n| Max LTV (borrow limit) | 70% | `MAX_LTV_BPS` |\n| Liquidation threshold | 85% | `LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS` |\n| Liquidation bonus | 5% | `LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS` |\n| Close factor | 50%, lifted to 100% below HF 0.95 | `CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS`, `FULL_LIQUIDATION_HEALTH_FACTOR` |\n| Interest rate | flat annual, settable, capped at 100% APR | `interestRateWad` |\n| Min debt per position | constructor arg (100 USDC in the deploy script) | `MIN_DEBT` |\n\nLTV and threshold are `constant`, so changing risk parameters means a redeploy plus a migration.\nThat is deliberate: it removes a whole class of \"owner rugs the risk params\" concerns. The rate\nand the oracle *are* mutable — see the operator section.\n\n## How health is computed\n\nEverything is valued in USD with 18 decimals. Both legs are priced — WETH **and** USDC — so a\nUSDC depeg moves the debt side instead of being silently assumed to be $1.\n\n```\ncollateralValue = collateralWETH * wethPriceUSD / 1e18          (rounded down)\ndebtValue       = debtUSDC       * usdcPriceUSD / 1e6           (rounded up)\n\nhealthFactor    = collateralValue * 8500 / 10000 * 1e18 / debtValue\n```\n\n- `healthFactor >= 1e18` → healthy.\n- `healthFactor < 1e18` → liquidatable. This is exactly \"debt exceeds 85% of collateral value\".\n- No debt → `type(uint256).max`.\n\nBorrowing and withdrawing are gated on a **different, stricter** check: after the action,\n`debtValue <= collateralValue * 70% `. So you can open a position at 70% LTV, and it does not\nbecome liquidatable until the ratio drifts to 85% — roughly an 18% drop in ETH price from a\nmaxed-out position. `withdrawCollateral` is checked against the 70% limit too, so a withdrawal\ncan never leave a position that is immediately liquidatable.\n\nInterest is a global borrow index. `accrueInterest()` runs at the top of every state-changing\nentry point:\n\n```\nborrowIndex *= (1 + rate * elapsed / 365 days)\n```\n\nA position stores `scaledDebt` (index shares); actual debt is `scaledDebt * borrowIndex / 1e18`,\nrounded up. Interest therefore costs O(1) and touches no per-user storage. The read-only views\n(`debtOf`, `healthFactor`, `totalDebt`, `maxBorrowable`, `previewLiquidation`) project the index\nforward to the current block, so they agree with what a transaction in this block would see.\n\nRounding is uniformly resolved in the protocol's favour: debt rounds up, collateral value rounds\ndown, borrow shares round up, repayment shares round down, seized collateral rounds down.\n\n## What a liquidator has to do\n\nCall `liquidate(account, repayAmount, to)`:\n\n1. Hold USDC and approve the pool for `repayAmount`.\n2. The position must have `healthFactor < 1e18` at the moment of execution. Use\n   `previewLiquidation(account, repayAmount)` to get the exact `(repaid, seized)` pair the\n   transaction will produce — it runs identical math.\n3. `repayAmount` is clamped to the close factor, so passing `type(uint256).max` is the normal\n   way to take the maximum currently available. The clamp is:\n   - HF ≥ 0.95 → at most 50% of the debt,\n   - HF < 0.95 → the entire debt,\n   - and always the entire debt if a 50% close would leave less than `MIN_DEBT` behind.\n4. Payout: `seized = repaid * usdcPrice / 1e6 * 1.05 * 1e18 / wethPrice`, transferred to `to`.\n   That is a 5% discount on the WETH relative to spot.\n\nFailure modes a bot should expect:\n\n- `PositionHealthy(hf)` — someone got there first, or the price moved back.\n- `DebtBelowMinimum` — the chosen `repayAmount` would leave dust; repay more (or pass `max`).\n- `CollateralExhausted` — the position has already been fully drained; nothing to take.\n- Oracle revert (`StalePrice` / `InvalidPrice`) — the whole market is frozen, including\n  liquidations. See the risk note below.\n\n**Underwater positions.** If the requested repayment would seize more WETH than the position\nholds, the pool takes all of it and scales the payment back to what that collateral is actually\nworth net of the bonus (`repaid = collateralValue / 1.05`). The liquidator is never asked to\noverpay. The residual debt is real bad debt: the position ends with zero collateral and non-zero\ndebt, and further `liquidate` calls revert with `CollateralExhausted`. The operator clears it\nwith `absorbBadDebt(account)`, which is pure bookkeeping — it stops phantom debt from inflating\n`totalDebt` and accruing interest forever. The loss was already realised.\n\nSelf-liquidation is allowed and is not a problem: the borrower pays USDC and gets back their own\nWETH at a 5% discount to itself, which is a wash minus gas.\n\n## What an operator has to get right on mainnet\n\n**Oracle configuration is the whole ballgame.**\n\n- Feeds must be `X/USD` for *both* tokens. The pool calls `getAssetPrice` on the collateral and\n  the debt token; a market deployed with only one feed configured will not function at all\n  (`setOracle` checks this, but the constructor takes the oracle on trust).\n- `heartbeat` must come from the feed's published heartbeat plus a margin. Mainnet at time of\n  writing: ETH/USD is 1h, USDC/USD is 24h. Setting these too tight causes spurious freezes;\n  too loose accepts stale prices. The deploy script uses 1h15m and 25h.\n- `minAnswer`/`maxAnswer` are a sanity band, not a risk control. Set them wide (the script uses\n  $100–$1,000,000 for ETH, $0.90–$1.10 for USDC). Note the USDC band means a depeg past 10%\n  freezes the market rather than liquidating everyone at a distressed price — that is the\n  intended behaviour, but it *is* a choice, and it means an operator decision is required to\n  unfreeze.\n- The oracle fails closed. **A dead feed halts liquidations too**, which is the standard money\n  market trade-off but should be monitored: alert on `latestRoundData().updatedAt` age\n  approaching the configured heartbeat, not after it passes.\n- Chainlink deprecates and re-deploys aggregator proxies. `setFeed` and `setOracle` exist for\n  this. Both are owner-only.\n\n**Ownership.**\n\n- Both contracts are `Ownable2Step`. The pool's owner is set in the constructor; the oracle's\n  ownership is transferred at the end of the deploy script and **the new owner must call\n  `acceptOwnership()`** or the deployer key remains in control. Verify this post-deploy.\n- The owner is a trusted role: it can change the interest rate (capped at 100% APR), swap the\n  oracle, pause, and move idle USDC. Use a multisig, and put a timelock in front of `setOracle`\n  and `setInterestRate` — an instant oracle swap is a complete compromise of the market.\n\n**Liquidity.**\n\n- There is deliberately **no depositor/share accounting**. USDC is supplied by the owner via\n  `fund()` and pulled back via `defund()`, which is bounded by the pool's free USDC balance.\n  Interest accrues into that balance. If this needs to accept third-party lenders, that is a\n  separate supply-side contract (ERC-4626 vault or an index-scaled deposit), not a tweak.\n- `defund` can drain all idle USDC, which would block new borrows. It cannot touch collateral —\n  that is a different token — and it cannot touch USDC that has already been lent out. Repayments\n  and liquidations always work regardless of the USDC balance.\n- Keep enough idle USDC that the market is usable; monitor `availableLiquidity()`.\n\n**Launch checklist.**\n\n- Fund the pool *before* announcing; `borrow` reverts with `InsufficientLiquidity` on an empty pool.\n- Set `MIN_DEBT` high enough that liquidating the smallest allowed position is profitable after\n  gas. At 100 USDC minimum and a 5% bonus, the smallest full liquidation pays ~$5 — thin at high\n  gas prices. Consider raising it, or accept that dust positions sit unliquidated until gas is\n  cheap. This is a constructor argument, so get it right at deploy time.\n- `pause()` blocks new deposits and new borrows only. Repay, withdraw and liquidate stay open by\n  design — pausing must not trap borrowers or stop the market from de-risking itself.\n\n**Known limitations, stated plainly.**\n\n- Interest compounds only when the pool is touched, so the effective rate is slightly below the\n  nominal one during quiet periods. Fine for a flat-rate market; do not port this accrual to a\n  utilisation-driven model without revisiting it.\n- Single collateral, single debt asset, no per-asset caps, no reserve factor, no supply side.\n- Bad debt is socialised onto the owner's capital (the pool simply holds less USDC than it lent).\n  With third-party lenders that would need explicit loss accounting.\n- No flash-loan / same-block price manipulation protection beyond using Chainlink. Chainlink\n  spot prices are not manipulable by a single transaction, but they *do* lag, and a liquidation\n  cascade during a fast move can leave bad debt. The 15-point gap between the 70% borrow limit\n  and the 85% threshold is the buffer; widen it if ETH volatility assumptions change.\n- WETH and USDC are both well-behaved (no fee-on-transfer, no rebasing), which the accounting\n  assumes. USDC is upgradeable and has a blocklist — a blocked borrower cannot be paid out by\n  `borrow`, and a blocked liquidator cannot pay. That is USDC's behaviour, not something this\n  contract can route around.\n- `absorbBadDebt` is owner-only and irreversible for that account. It does not move funds.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":477,"cache_read_input_tokens":69211,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":477},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"0a77c333-d0ad-4a46-a1ea-f54d6c1e4ac9","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:36:34.434Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTvxTWxmYSMtT4LDZtP"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU1vb3eQAiWdSyqzYM6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `forge build` compiles clean, `forge test` is 30/30.\n\n## What's there\n\n**`src/LendingPool.sol`** — the market. WETH in, USDC out, 70% borrow LTV / 85% liquidation threshold / 5% bonus, flat-rate interest via a global borrow index (per-user debt stored as index shares, so accrual is O(1)).\n\n**`src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol`** — `X/USD` feeds normalised to 1e18. Fails closed on stale, zero/negative, or out-of-band answers.\n\n**`script/Deploy.s.sol`** — mainnet WETH/USDC + ETH-USD/USDC-USD aggregator proxies wired up, with heartbeats taken from the feeds' published values.\n\n**`NOTES.md`** — health math, the liquidator's playbook (including failure modes and the underwater case), and the operator section.\n\n## Decisions worth flagging\n\n- **Both legs are priced.** USDC value comes from the USDC/USD feed rather than being assumed to be $1, so a depeg moves the debt side instead of being invisible.\n- **No supply side.** The task describes only the borrower side, so USDC liquidity is owner-funded via `fund`/`defund` (bounded by free balance — it can't touch collateral or lent-out principal). Adding third-party lenders is a separate contract, not a tweak; called out in NOTES.\n- **Close factor 50%, lifted to 100% below HF 0.95** — and lifted whenever a partial close would strand sub-`MIN_DEBT` dust. Without that second rule a 50%-capped liquidation on a small position would be permanently blocked by the min-debt check.\n- **Bad debt is explicit.** When the requested repayment would seize more WETH than exists, the pool takes all of it and scales the liquidator's payment down to `collateralValue / 1.05` — the liquidator never overpays. The residual is real bad debt, cleared by owner-only `absorbBadDebt` (bookkeeping only, moves no funds).\n- **LTV and threshold are `constant`.** Changing risk parameters means a redeploy. The rate and oracle are mutable — NOTES recommends a timelock on both, since an instant oracle swap is a total compromise.\n- **`pause()` blocks deposits and borrows only.** Repay, withdraw, and liquidate stay open so a pause can't trap borrowers or stop the market de-risking.\n\nThe one real operational sharp edge, documented in NOTES: the oracle failing closed means a dead feed halts liquidations too. That's the standard trade-off, but it needs monitoring on feed age *before* the heartbeat lapses, not after."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":258,"cache_read_input_tokens":74596,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":258},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"210bce02-a59d-4eba-919b-502846dc088f","uuid":"ff2f2af9-42f8-43e2-9457-68090723f5cf","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:02.396Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU1tntrtQZY9E9uNkLG"}
